EXTRAORDINARY

අංක 2469/29– 2026 ජනවාɜ මස 01 වැǧ ȯහස්පƯǦදා – 2026.01.01අංක 2469/29– 2026 ජනවාɜ මස 01 වැǧ ȯහස්පƯǦදා – 2026.01.01

No. 2469/29 – THURSDAY, JANUARY 01, 2025

(Published by Authority)

PART IV (A) – PROVINCIAL COUNCILS

Local Government Notification

Ayagama Pradeshiya Sabha

AS per the function assigned to the local councils in Sabaragamuwa Province from “the order of the preparation of plans of roads and narrow lanes maintained by Pradeshiya Sabha in Sabaragamuwa Province and publication of notices showing the list of names” in Extra ordinary Gazette No. 2316/20 and dated 24th January 2023 published by the Governor of Sabaragamuwa Province which should be read with Section 24 of Pradeshiya Sabha Act, No.15 of 1987, it is hereby notice to the public that the roads and narrow lanes included in the schedule below should be declared as the roads and narrow lanes owned by the Ayagama Pradeshiya Sabha at its meeting under the decision No. (06) 01 .36 - II and dated 11.12.2025.

And it is further notice that if it is the opinion of the party who is entitled to the said land, that any road or narrow lane included in the said schedule go through, a case should be ﬁled in the court before the expiry of a period of three months from the date of this notice to prove the entitlement.

And it is further noticed that every road and narrow lane included in the said schedule that cases are not assigned before the expiry of the said period of three months, are considered as a road or narrow lane that owned by Ayagama Pradeshiya Sabha.

A.M.C.K.Senewirathna, Secretary, Ayagama Pradeshiya Sabha.

Ayagama Pradeshiya Sabha,Ayagama.10th of December 2025.
